# Zeovus Life — Developer Reference / Product Category Database

> Complete Markdown transcription of `Zeovus_Life_Category_Tables_GENERIC.xlsx`.
> This document intentionally includes the workbook index, methodology/corrections, every category sheet, and the complete 268-SKU master mapping.

## Source & Verification

- Source workbook: `Zeovus_Life_Category_Tables_GENERIC.xlsx`
- Source reference stated in workbook: Zeovus supplement brochure (`1783518365511_supplement_brochure.pdf`).
- Workbook states that Key Actives were cross-checked against the source PDF page images.
- Total unique formulations: **268**.

### Corrections Applied in This Version
1. Beauty from Within — 6 products had Key Actives shifted by one row position during original data entry (a label/ingredient
column-alignment error). Verified against the source PDF's 'Nutri-Cosmetics' table (page 17) and corrected:
Meltos Hair Loss Strip, Meltos Anti-Wrinkle Strip, Meltos Inner Calm and Beauty Strip, Meltos Glowing Skin Strip,
Meltos Anti-Acne Strip, Meltos Healthy Skin Hair & Nails Strip.
2. Promising Herbs — 'Triphala' actives corrected from a generic placeholder to the actual listed composition
(Amla, Haritaki, Bibhitaki) per the source PDF (page 21).
3. All other ~260 formulations were individually re-verified against the source PDF page images and confirmed correct.
Rows corrected in this version are marked 'CORRECTED' in the Status column of their category sheet.

### Manufacturing Format & DDS Columns — Methodology
Two new columns were added: 'Feasible Manufacturing Formats' and 'Recommended DDS / Delivery Technology'.
These were assigned using pharmaceutical/nutraceutical formulation science principles applied to each product's
actual verified ingredient list — not generically copy-pasted. Key reasoning drivers per product:
- Lipophilicity: fat-soluble actives (omega-3, CoQ10, vitamin D/E/K2, astaxanthin, curcumin) suit softgels,
liquid emulsions, and lipid-based DDS (nanoemulsion, SEDDS/SMEDDS, solid lipid nanoparticles).
- Water solubility: B-vitamins, vitamin C, minerals suit tablets, effervescent, ODS, sublingual formats.
- Dose size: gram-scale actives (whey protein, collagen peptides) are restricted to powder/RTD — a capsule
or gummy cannot physically hold a 20-30g dose.
- Live-organism status: probiotic strains are restricted away from gummies/effervescent tablets (heat/moisture
kill bacteria) and pointed toward enteric-coated capsules and microencapsulation instead.
- Known bioavailability constraints: curcumin, CoQ10, and glutathione have well-documented poor oral
bioavailability in the pharmaceutical literature, so phytosome, liposomal, SMEDDS, and sublingual routes
are specifically flagged for these actives.
- Transdermal patches (including microneedle patches) are noted as a genuine, fast-growing commercial
category (est. USD 7.3B market in 2024) for select small, potent actives: B12, biotin, D3/K2, iron,
magnesium, and melatonin — but NOT for bulk minerals, large peptides, herbal blends, or probiotics,
where transdermal absorption is not physiologically realistic.
This is a formulation-science feasibility assessment for R&D/product-development planning, not a claim that
Zeovus currently manufactures every format listed — it flags what is scientifically achievable.

## 4. Developer Implementation Notes

- Treat the **MASTER MAPPING** section as the canonical one-row-per-SKU dataset.
- Treat the category sheets as category-specific views of the same formulation catalog.
- Preserve product names, brand lines, actives, primary/secondary categories, manufacturing formats, DDS technologies, and status exactly unless a deliberate product-data change is requested.
- The workbook uses `|` as a multi-value separator inside several cells. In this Markdown transcription those internal separators are escaped as `\|` so the values remain a single Markdown table cell.
- `Verified` means the workbook marks the formulation as verified against the source PDF.
- `CORRECTED` marks rows specifically identified by the workbook as corrected during this version's verification process.
- `—` means no secondary benefit/category was specified in the workbook.
